sheet Start Here | cell A3 text "1. On Register, paste one contribution line per row over the example rows, in the eleven canonical columns the command line tool reads and the importer writes. Dates are real Excel dates; amounts are dollars; yes/no columns take yes, no or blank (no)." | fmt="General" align{vertical=top wrapText}
sheet Start Here | cell A4 text "2. On Summary, set the as-at date (notional earnings on unpaid lines run to it), an assessment date if the ATO has assessed, and the two confirmations the command line tool asks for: LCR 2026/1 transition allocation and remittance-only review." | fmt="General" align{vertical=top wrapText}
sheet Start Here | cell A5 text "3. Read the calculated columns on Register: pathway, final deadline, verdict (ON_TIME, AT_RISK, LATE, UNPAID, UNKNOWN, SKIPPED), the conservative outer outcomes where the facts cannot decide, days late, final shortfall after s 18D, notional earnings, the uplift range and the experimental SG charge estimate range." | fmt="General" align{vertical=top wrapText}
sheet Start Here | cell A6 text "4. Review Checks: BLOCKED means the register cannot be read or a required confirmation is missing. REVIEW means a line is exposed, a line is undecided, or the file cannot produce ON_TIME and remittance-only review is not confirmed. PASS mirrors the command line tool's exit code 0." | fmt="General" align{vertical=top wrapText}
sheet Start Here | cell A7 text "5. The statutory test is receipt by the fund. A remittance date alone gives AT_RISK, never ON_TIME. Fill fund_received_date from the clearing house or fund before treating a verdict as final." | fmt="General" align{vertical=top wrapText}
sheet Start Here | cell A8 text "6. Experimental review aid. Not a compliance determination. The ATO makes the assessment; choice loading, the maximum contributions base and post-assessment charges are not estimated." | fmt="General" align{vertical=top wrapText}
